## 2026-09-16 — the continuation on the Mac mini: one row per seat exit

**Column shape.** The header at `LEDGER.md:5` declares four columns; every row written since has five —
`seat · model · kind · detail · tokens`. The rows below follow the FIVE-column shape the file actually
uses. (The header/row drift is a record defect of the mission's own line, named here, not fixed —
rewriting 480 historical rows is not this seat's contract.)

**Times are COMMIT timestamps, not the orchestrator's dispatch estimates.** Every task's packet was
committed immediately before its dispatch and every seat ended in a records commit, so `dispatched` =
the packet commit's time and `exited` = the seat's last records commit's time. Both are entailed;
the SDD ledger's "~03:3x"-style times are approximations and are not reproduced.

**How `SKILLS LOADED` was verified — read this once, it applies to every row marked `SL:handoff`.**
By the orchestrator, from the seat's handoff line; **the transcript body was not grepped.** I measured
the reports themselves this pass: of the **20** `agent-reports/cont-t*.md` self-reports, exactly **2**
contain the string `SKILLS LOADED` — `cont-t8-stub-class.md:3` (a real handoff-shaped line) and
`cont-t5-entrypoint-inventory.md:122` (prose ABOUT the discipline, not a declaration). So for 18 of 20
seats the only evidence of the line is the handoff message the orchestrator read at exit. STRENGTH:
consistent-with for the seats, entailed for the count.

**Verdicts are the ORCHESTRATOR's review lines** in the SDD ledger, not the seats' self-assessments
(D67). Where a seat's own claim is quoted it is marked "seat claims".

### Rulings taken on V's behalf during this continuation

Each is a decision the orchestrator made where V's own answer was owed. Each carries a veto window and
a row in `V-DECISIONS-PACKET.md`.

**Ruling:** attribution is per ASSERTION, by blob hash — never per suite (Task 3; plan `913add73`; ledger :41, :44). STRENGTH: entailed.
**Ruling:** retirement is per assertion — a suite is deleted only when every assertion has a live home, and non-web assertions are re-homed VERBATIM (Task 1; ledger :26, :28). STRENGTH: entailed.
**Ruling:** a render site that never existed on either parent is V's UI program's work, outside the Scope law; the rows are ACCEPTED REDS with tickets, not merge debt (Tasks 3 and 4; ledger :41, :83). STRENGTH: entailed.
